\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b_{1}^{a_{1}}}{\Gamma(a_{1})}\theta^{-(a_{1}+1)}\mathrm{e}^{-b_{1}/\theta}\int_{n^{\rho_{22}}\theta}^{\infty}\frac{b_{2}^{a_{2}}}{\Gamma(a_{2})}\tau^{-(a_{2}+1)}\mathrm{e}^{-b_{2}/\tau}\mathrm{d}\tau\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{n^{-\rho_{22}/2}}\frac{b_{1}^{a_{1}}}{\Gamma(a_{1})}\theta^{-(a_{1}+1)}\mathrm{e}^{-b_{1}/\theta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{b_{2}^{a_{2}}}{\Gamma(a_{2})}\tau^{-(a_{2}+1)}\mathrm{e}^{-b_{2}/\tau}\mathrm{d}\tau\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{n^{-\rho_{22}/2}}^{\infty}\frac{b_{1}^{a_{1}}}{\Gamma(a_{1})}\theta^{-(a_{1}+1)}\mathrm{e}^{-b_{1}/\theta}\int_{n^{\rho_{22}/2}}^{\infty}\frac{b_{2}^{a_{2}}}{\Gamma(a_{2})}\tau^{-(a_{2}+1)}\mathrm{e}^{-b_{2}/\tau}\mathrm{d}\tau\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{n^{-\rho_{22}/2}}\frac{b_{1}^{a_{1}}}{\Gamma(a_{1})}\theta^{-(a_{1}+1)}\mathrm{e}^{-b_{1}/\theta}\mathrm{d}\theta+\int_{n^{\rho_{22}/2}}^{\infty}\frac{b_{2}^{a_{2}}}{\Gamma(a_{2})}\tau^{-(a_{2}+1)}\mathrm{e}^{-b_{2}/\tau}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}\int_{n^{\rho_{22}/2}}^{\infty}\frac{b_{1}^{a_{1}}}{\Gamma(a_{1})}t^{a_{1}-1}\mathrm{e}^{-b_{1}t}\mathrm{d}t+\int_{n^{\rho_{22}/2}}^{\infty}\frac{b_{2}^{a_{2}}}{\Gamma(a_{2})}\tau^{-(a_{2}+1)}\mathrm{e}^{-b_{2}/\tau}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\int_{n^{\rho_{22}/2}}^{\infty}\frac{b_{1}^{a_{1}}}{\Gamma(a_{1})}\mathrm{e}^{-b_{1}t/2}\mathrm{d}t+\int_{n^{\rho_{22}/2}}^{\infty}\frac{b_{2}^{a_{2}}}{\Gamma(a_{2})}\tau^{-(a_{2}+1)}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle=\frac{2b_{1}^{a_{1}-1}}{\Gamma(a_{1})}\exp\big(-b_{1}n^{\rho_{22}/2}/2\big)+\frac{b_{2}^{a_{2}}}{a_{2}\Gamma(a_{2})}n^{-a_{2}\rho_{22}/2}

\displaystyle\prec n^{-(3p+6)},

(S.66)

if
a_{2}\rho_{22}/2>3p+6
, or
a_{2}>2(3p+6)/\rho_{22}
, where in (i) we use the change of variable
t=1/\theta
.

Fourth, the inverse Gaussian prior on
\alpha
has the density for
\alpha\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
,

\pi(\alpha)=\left(\frac{\lambda}{2\pi\alpha^{3}}\right)^{1/2}\exp\left\{-\frac{\lambda(\alpha-\mu)^{2}}{2\mu^{2}\alpha}\right\}.

For the left tail of
\alpha
, we have that

\displaystyle\Pi(\alpha<n^{-\rho_{31}})

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{n^{-\rho_{31}}}\left(\frac{\lambda}{2\pi\alpha^{3}}\right)^{1/2}\exp\left(-\frac{\lambda\alpha}{2\mu^{2}}+\frac{\lambda}{\mu}-\frac{\lambda}{2\alpha}\right)\mathrm{d}\alpha

\displaystyle\leq\{\lambda/(2\pi)\}^{1/2}\mathrm{e}^{\lambda/\mu}\int_{0}^{n^{-\rho_{31}}}\alpha^{-3/2}\exp\big\{-\lambda/(2\alpha)\big\}\mathrm{d}\alpha

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}\{\lambda/(2\pi)\}^{1/2}\mathrm{e}^{\lambda/\mu}\int_{n^{\rho_{31}}}^{\infty}t^{-1/2}\exp\big\{-\lambda t/2\big\}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\{2/(\lambda\pi)\}^{1/2}\mathrm{e}^{\lambda/\mu}\exp\big(-\lambda n^{\rho_{31}}/2\big)

\displaystyle\prec n^{-(3p+6)},

(S.67)

where in (i) we use the change of variable
t=1/\alpha
.

Similarly, for the right tail of
\alpha
, we have that

\displaystyle\Pi(\alpha>n^{\rho_{32}})

\displaystyle=\int_{n^{\rho_{32}}}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\lambda}{2\pi\alpha^{3}}\right)^{1/2}\exp\left(-\frac{\lambda\alpha}{2\mu^{2}}+\frac{\lambda}{\mu}-\frac{\lambda}{2\alpha}\right)\mathrm{d}\alpha

\displaystyle\leq\{\lambda/(2\pi)\}^{1/2}\mathrm{e}^{\lambda/\mu}\int_{n^{\rho_{32}}}^{\infty}\exp\big\{-\lambda\alpha/(2\mu^{2})\big\}\mathrm{d}\alpha

\displaystyle\leq\{2/(\lambda\pi)\}^{1/2}\mu^{2}\mathrm{e}^{\lambda/\mu}\exp\big\{-\lambda n^{\rho_{32}}/(2\mu^{2})\big\}

\displaystyle\prec n^{-(3p+6)}.

(S.68)

Finally, we combine (
S6
), (
S6
), (
S6
), (
S6
) and (
S6
) with (
S.63
) to conclude that
\Pi(\Ecal_{n}^{c})\leq n^{-(3p+6)}
for all sufficiently large
n
. Hence, Assumption
5
is satisfied.
∎

S7
Proof of Theorem
3

The proof of Theorem
3
is long and we proceed in several steps. We first present the Taylor series expansion for Matérn covariance function. The Matérn covariance function
\theta K_{\alpha,\nu}
defined in (
2
) can be expressed as the sum of an infinite series, whose formula depends whether or not
\nu
is an integer. The following expansion of
\theta K_{\alpha,\nu}(\mathbf{s}-\mathbf{t})
for
\mathbf{s},\mathbf{t}\in[0,1]^{d}
can be found on page 2772 of
Loh 2015
.

\displaystyle\theta K_{\alpha,\nu}(\mathbf{s}-\mathbf{t})=\textstyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big\{\zeta_{j}\|\mathbf{s}-\mathbf{t}\|^{2j}+\zeta^{*}_{\nu+j}G_{\nu+j}(\|\mathbf{s}-\mathbf{t}\|)\big\},

(S.69)

where
\mathbb{Z}_{+}
is the set of all positive integers, and

\displaystyle G_{s}(t)

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}t^{2s}\log(t),&\text{ if }s\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},\\
t^{2s},&\text{ if }s\notin\mathbb{Z}_{+}.\end{array}\right.

The terms of
\zeta_{j}
and
\zeta^{*}_{\nu+j}
for
j=0,1,2,\ldots
are defined as follows:

If
\nu\not\in\mathbb{Z}
, then

\displaystyle\zeta_{j}

\displaystyle=\theta\alpha^{2j-2\nu}\xi_{j},\qquad\xi_{j}=1/\big\{2^{2j}j!\textstyle\prod_{i=1}^{j}(i-\nu)\big\},

\displaystyle\zeta^{*}_{\nu+j}

\displaystyle=\theta\alpha^{2j}\xi^{*}_{\nu+j},\qquad\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}=-\pi/\left\{2^{2j+2\nu}j!\Gamma(j+1+\nu)\Gamma(\nu)\sin(\nu\pi)\right\}.

(S.72)

If
\nu\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
, then

\displaystyle\zeta_{j}=\theta\alpha^{2j-2\nu}(-1)^{j}\frac{(\nu-j-1)!}{2^{2j}(\nu-1)!j!},\quad\text{if ~}j<\nu,

\displaystyle\zeta_{j}=\theta\alpha^{2j-2\nu}\left\{\xi_{1,j}+\xi_{2,j}\log(\alpha)\right\},~{\rm if~}j\geq\nu,~\text{ where }

\displaystyle\xi_{1,j}=\frac{(-1)^{\nu}\left[\psi(j-\nu+1)+\psi(j+1)+2\log 2\right]}{2^{2j}(j-\nu)!j!(\nu-1)!},\quad\xi_{2,j}=\frac{(-1)^{\nu+1}}{2^{2j-1}(j-\nu)!j!(\nu-1)!},

\displaystyle\zeta^{*}_{\nu+j}=\theta\alpha^{2j}\xi^{*}_{\nu+j},\quad\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}=\frac{(-1)^{\nu+1}}{2^{2\nu+2j-1}(\nu-1)!j!(\nu+j)!},

(S.73)

where
\psi(\cdot)
is the digamma function. For both cases, the coefficients
\xi^{*}_{\nu+j},\xi_{1,j},\xi_{2,j}
for all
j=0,1,\ldots
are all upper bounded by constant.

We then cite an important lemma about the series of
m
-dependent constants
c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}
and
c_{d,\ell}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}
as defined in Lemma
1
of the main text. As
n=m^{d}\to\infty
(and so
m\to\infty
), the
m
-dependent constants
c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}
are uniformly close to a deterministic sequence of
m
-independent constants
c_{d,\ell}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}
which satisfy certain relations, as shown by Lemma 2 and Corollary 2 of
Loh
et al. 2021
.

Lemma S.9
.

(Lemma 2 and Corollary 2 of
Loh
et al. 2021
) Let
d,\ell\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
. Let
\mathbf{i}=(i_{1},\ldots,i_{d})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}
where
1\leq i_{1},\ldots,i_{d}\leq m-\ell\omega_{m}
. Then there exists a deterministic sequence of
m
-independent constants
c_{d,\ell}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}
, such that
c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}
in Lemma
1
of the main text satisfies

\displaystyle c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}=c_{d,\ell}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1}),\quad\text{for all }0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell,

as
m\to\infty
, where the term
O(\omega_{m}^{-1})
is uniform over all
1\leq i_{1},\ldots,i_{d}\leq m-\ell\omega_{m}
and any
\delta_{\mathbf{i};k}\in[0,1)
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell}c_{d,\ell}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}k_{d}^{\ell}=\ell!,

and

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell}c_{d,\ell}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\cdot\prod_{j=1}^{d}k_{j}^{l_{j}}=0,

for all integers
l_{1},\ldots,l_{d}
satisfying
0\leq l_{1},\ldots,l_{d-1}\leq\ell
,
0\leq l_{d}\leq\ell-1
, and
0\leq l_{1}+\ldots+l_{d}\leq\ell
.

In the rest of the proof, we use
C
to denote a generic positive constant that can take different values at different places, and
C
only depends on
\nu,d,p,\theta_{0},\alpha_{0},\tau_{0},\beta_{0}
. The order notation
O(\cdot)
and
o(\cdot)
is uniform over all
m
. For abbreviation, we write
\mathbb{P}_{0}=\mathbb{P}_{(\theta_{0},\alpha_{0},\tau_{0},\beta_{0})}
, use
\mathbb{E}_{0}
to denote the expectation under
\mathbb{P}_{0}
, and use
\mathbb{E}
to denote the expectation under
\mathbb{P}_{(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)}
for a generic parameter vector
(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)
. We will assume that Assumptions
1
,
6
and
7
hold throughout this section.

Proof of
Theorem
3
.

Proof of Part (i):

By definition, we have that
(\theta_{0},\alpha_{0},\tau_{0},\beta_{0})\in\Ecal_{n}
as
n\to\infty
. From (
S.89
) and (
S.96
) in Section
S7.1
, for any given
\epsilon_{1}>0
, we have that uniformly for all
(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)\in\Ecal_{n}
, for all sufficiently large
n
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)/(\theta g_{\ell_{\star},\nu})-1\right|<\epsilon_{1}/4,

(S.74)

with the expectation taken under
\mathbb{P}_{(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)}
.

Under the true measure
\mathbb{P}_{0}
, for any
\epsilon_{1}\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\quad~\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\left|\frac{\widehat{\theta}_{n}}{\theta_{0}}-1\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\left|\frac{\mathbb{E}_{0}\left(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}{\theta_{0}g_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}\right|\cdot\left|\frac{V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}}{\mathbb{E}_{0}\left(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}-1\right|+\left|\frac{\mathbb{E}_{0}\left(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}{\theta_{0}g_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}-1\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\left|\frac{V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}}{\mathbb{E}_{0}\left(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}-1\right|>\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{5}\right).

(S.75)

Similarly, on the set
\Bcal^{c}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2})\cap\Ecal_{n}
, since
|\theta/\theta_{0}-1|\geq\epsilon_{1}
, for all sufficiently large
n
such that (
S.74
) holds for all
(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)\in\Ecal_{n}
, we have that for any
\epsilon_{1}\in(0,1/2)
,

\displaystyle\quad~\sup_{\Bcal^{c}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2})\cap\Ecal_{n}}\mathbb{P}_{(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)}\left(\left|\frac{\widehat{\theta}_{n}}{\theta_{0}}-1\right|\leq\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Bcal^{c}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2})\cap\Ecal_{n}}\mathbb{P}_{(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)}\left(\left|\frac{\theta}{\theta_{0}}-1\right|-\frac{\theta}{\theta_{0}}\left|\frac{\widehat{\theta}_{n}}{\theta}-1\right|\leq\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Bcal^{c}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2})\cap\Ecal_{n}}\mathbb{P}_{(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)}\left(\left|\frac{\widehat{\theta}_{n}}{\theta}-1\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{2(1+\epsilon_{1})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Bcal^{c}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2})\cap\Ecal_{n}}\mathbb{P}_{(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)}\left(\left|\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}{\theta g_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}\right|\cdot\left|\frac{V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}}{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}-1\right|+\left|\frac{\mathbb{E}(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}})}{\theta g_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}-1\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\Bcal^{c}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2})\cap\Ecal_{n}}\mathbb{P}_{(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)}\left(\left|\frac{V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}}{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}-1\right|>\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{15}\right).

(S.76)

From (
S7.2
) in Section
S7.2
, we have that uniformly for all
(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)\in\Ecal_{n}
, there exists
c_{1}>0
, such that

\displaystyle\quad~\mathbb{P}_{(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)}\left(\left|\frac{V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}}{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}-1\right|>\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{15}\right)\leq\exp\left\{-c_{1}\varphi\left(n^{b_{1}^{\prime}}\epsilon_{1}\right)\right\},

\displaystyle\text{where }b_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\min\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2(1-\gamma)\nu}{d}-\rho_{22},~\frac{1-\gamma}{2}-\frac{\log\log n}{\log n},~\frac{1}{4}+\frac{(1-\gamma)(\ell_{\star}-2\nu)}{d}-\frac{\rho_{1}+\rho_{22}}{2},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\frac{1-\gamma}{4}+\frac{(1-\gamma)(\ell_{\star}-\nu)}{d}-\frac{\rho_{1}}{2}-\frac{\log\log n}{2\log n}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\geq\min\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2(1-\gamma)\nu}{d}-\rho_{22},~\frac{1-\gamma}{2}-\varsigma,~\frac{1}{4}+\frac{(1-\gamma)(\ell_{\star}-2\nu)}{d}-\frac{\rho_{1}+\rho_{22}}{2},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\frac{1-\gamma}{4}+\frac{(1-\gamma)(\ell_{\star}-\nu)}{d}-\frac{\rho_{1}}{2}-\varsigma\Bigg\}=b_{1},

(S.77)

for an arbitrarily small
\varsigma>0
as
n\to\infty
.
Therefore, (
S7
), (
S7
) and (
S7
) implies that (
7
) and (
9
) in Assumption
4
are satisfied with
b_{1}
given in (
(i)
).

Proof of Part (ii):

Similar to the proof of Part (i), for
\widehat{\tau}_{n}
, by using (
S7.1
) and (
S.98
) from Section
S7.1
, we obtain that for any
\epsilon_{2}\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\left|\frac{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}{\tau_{0}}-1\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon_{2}}{2}\right)\leq\mathbb{P}_{0}\left(\left|\frac{V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}}{\mathbb{E}_{0}\left(V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}-1\right|>\frac{\epsilon_{2}}{5}\right),

\displaystyle\sup_{\Bcal^{c}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2})\cap\Ecal_{n}}\mathbb{P}_{(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)}\left(\left|\frac{\widehat{\tau}_{n}}{\tau_{0}}-1\right|\leq\frac{\epsilon_{2}}{2}\right)\leq\sup_{\Bcal^{c}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2})\cap\Ecal_{n}}\mathbb{P}_{(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)}\left(\left|\frac{V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}}{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}-1\right|>\frac{\epsilon_{2}}{15}\right).

(S.78)

From (
S7.2
) in Section
S7.2
, we have that for any
\epsilon_{2}\in(0,1/2)
, uniformly for all
(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)\in\Ecal_{n}
, there exists
c_{2}>0
, such that

\displaystyle\quad~\mathbb{P}_{(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)}\left(\left|\frac{V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}}{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}-1\right|>\frac{\epsilon_{2}}{15}\right)\leq\exp\left\{-c_{2}\varphi\left(n^{b_{2}^{\prime}}\epsilon_{2}\right)\right\},

\displaystyle\text{where }b_{2}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\min\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2},~\frac{(1-\gamma)(4\nu+d)}{2d}-\rho_{21}-\frac{\log\log n}{\log n},

\displaystyle\qquad~~\frac{1}{4}+\frac{(1-\gamma)\ell_{\star}}{d}-\frac{\rho_{1}+\rho_{21}}{2},~\frac{(1-\gamma)(4\nu+d+4\ell_{\star})}{4d}-\frac{\rho_{1}+2\rho_{21}}{2}-\frac{\log\log n}{2\log n}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\geq\min\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2},~\frac{(1-\gamma)(4\nu+d)}{2d}-\rho_{21}-\varsigma,

\displaystyle\qquad~~\frac{1}{4}+\frac{(1-\gamma)\ell_{\star}}{d}-\frac{\rho_{1}+\rho_{21}}{2},~\frac{(1-\gamma)(4\nu+d+4\ell_{\star})}{4d}-\frac{\rho_{1}+2\rho_{21}}{2}-\varsigma\Bigg\}=b_{2},

(S.79)

for an arbitrarily small
\varsigma>0
as
n\to\infty
.
Therefore, (
S7
) and (
S7
) implies that (
8
) and (
10
) in Assumption
4
are satisfied with
b_{2}
given in (
(ii)
).
∎

The rest of this section includes several auxiliary technical results. Section
S7.1
presents the derivation for the uniform upper bounds of
\mathbb{E}(V_{u,d,\ell_{\star}})
for
u\in\{0,1\}
and all
(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)\in\Ecal_{n}
. Section
S7.2
presents the uniform error bounds for
V_{u,d,\ell_{\star}}/\mathbb{E}\left(V_{u,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)-1
for
u\in\{0,1\}
and all
(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)\in\Ecal_{n}
, which has been used in the proof of Theorem
3
above. Section
S7.3
includes the derivation for the Frobenius norms of some matrices that are used in Section
S7.2
. Section
S7.4
presents a technical lemma on the derivative of Matérn covariance function that is used in Section
S7.2
.

S7.1
Uniform Bounds for
\mathbb{E}(V_{u,d,\ell_{\star}})
on
\Ecal_{n}

We first derive some useful bounds for
\mathbb{E}(V_{u,d,\ell_{\star}})
for
u\in\{0,1\}
and all
(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)\in\Ecal_{n}
. Recall that
\ell_{\star}=\lceil\nu+d/2\rceil
. For short, we write
\mathrm{m}(\mathbf{s})=\mathrm{f}(\mathbf{s})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\beta
for
\mathbf{s}\in[0,1]^{d}
as the mean function of
Y(\cdot)
. In the following derivation, we always write
\mathbf{k}_{1}=(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}
and
\mathbf{k}_{2}=(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}
for
k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\in\mathbb{N}
.

From Lemma
1
of the main text, we have that for any integer
0\leq\ell\leq\ell_{\star}-1
, for any
\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}
for
u\in\{0,1\}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}\left\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})\right\|^{2\ell}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left[s_{j}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-s_{j}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})\right]^{2}\right\}^{\ell}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle 0,

(S.80)

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}\left\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})\right\|^{2\ell_{\star}}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\frac{(-1)^{\ell_{\star}}(2\ell_{\star})!}{(\ell_{\star}!)^{2}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}s_{j}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})^{\ell_{\star}}s_{j}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})^{\ell_{\star}}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(-1)^{\ell_{\star}}(2\ell_{\star})!\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}.

(S.81)

These two formulas will be useful in the following derivation.

We observe that

\displaystyle\quad~\mathbb{E}(V_{u,d,\ell_{\star}})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}\mathbb{E}\left\{Y(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m}))Y(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m}))\right\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}\mathrm{m}\big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})\big)\mathrm{m}\big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}\left(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}\theta K_{\alpha,\nu}\Big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\Big).

(S.82)

For the first term in (
S7.1
), since the partial derivatives of
\mathrm{f}_{1},\ldots,\mathrm{f}_{p}
up to the order
\lceil\nu+d/2\rceil\geq\ell_{\star}
are all upper bounded by
C_{\mathrm{f}}^{\prime}
by Assumption
7
, we can apply the Taylor series expansion to the mean function
\mathrm{m}(\cdot)
to obtain that

\displaystyle\quad~\left|\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\mathrm{m}\big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})\big)\right|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg\{\sum_{a_{1}+\ldots+a_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}-1}\frac{\mathrm{m}^{(a_{1},\ldots,a_{d})}(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}))}{a_{1}!\ldots a_{d}!}\prod_{j=1}^{d}[s_{j}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-s_{j}(\mathbf{i})]^{a_{j}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{a_{1}+\ldots+a_{d}=\ell_{\star}}\frac{\ell_{\star}}{a_{1}!\ldots a_{d}!}\prod_{j=1}^{d}[s_{j}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-s_{j}(\mathbf{i})]^{a_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{\ell_{\star}-1}

\displaystyle\quad\times\mathrm{m}^{(a_{1},\ldots,a_{d})}\Big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i})+t\big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i})\big)\Big)\mathrm{d}t\Bigg\}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}\Bigg|\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg\{\sum_{a_{1}+\ldots+a_{d}=\ell_{\star}}\frac{\ell_{\star}}{a_{1}!\ldots a_{d}!}\prod_{j=1}^{d}[s_{j}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-s_{j}(\mathbf{i})]^{a_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{\ell_{\star}-1}

\displaystyle\quad\times\mathrm{m}^{(a_{1},\ldots,a_{d})}\Big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i})+t\big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i})\big)\Big)\mathrm{d}t\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg\{\sum_{a_{1}+\ldots+a_{d}=\ell_{\star}}\frac{\ell_{\star}}{a_{1}!\ldots a_{d}!}\prod_{j=1}^{d}[s_{j}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})-s_{j}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1})]^{a_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{\ell_{\star}-1}

\displaystyle\quad\times\mathrm{m}^{(a_{1},\ldots,a_{d})}\Big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1})+t\big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1})\big)\Big)\mathrm{d}t\Bigg\}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{\leq}}CC_{\mathrm{f}}^{\prime}\|\beta\|_{1}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{\ell_{\star}},

(S.83)

as
n\rightarrow\infty
for some
C>0
dependent on
\ell_{\star},\nu,d
, where
\|\beta\|_{1}=\sum_{j=1}^{p}|\beta_{j}|
; the equation (i) follows from Lemma
1
of the main text; the inequality (ii) follows from that
\big|\mathrm{m}^{(a_{1},\ldots,a_{d})}(\mathbf{s})\big|\leq C_{\mathrm{f}}^{\prime}\|\beta\|_{1}
by Assumption
1
,
\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{\ell_{\star}-1}\mathrm{d}t\leq 1
, and Lemma
1
of the main text. Therefore,

\displaystyle\quad~\left|\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}\mathrm{m}\big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})\big)\mathrm{m}\big(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})\big)\right|

\displaystyle\leq CC_{\mathrm{f}}^{{}^{\prime}2}\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}|\Xi_{u,m}|\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}\leq CC_{\mathrm{f}}^{{}^{\prime}2}\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}m^{d}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}.

(S.84)

Now we turn to the second and third terms in (
S7.1
). We consider two cases, either
\nu\notin\mathbb{Z}
or
\nu\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
. Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{u,d,\ell}(\nu)

\displaystyle=\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}

\displaystyle\quad\times G_{\nu}\Big(\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|\Big),

(S.85)

where
G_{\nu}
is as defined in (
S7
).

Case A1.
If
\nu\notin\mathbb{Z}
, then using (
S.81
), we observe that

\displaystyle\quad~\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}})^{2}+\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\theta K_{\alpha,\nu}\left(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\right)

\displaystyle=\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}})^{2}+\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big\{\zeta_{j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2j}

\displaystyle\quad+\zeta^{*}_{\nu+j}G_{\nu+j}\Big(\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|\Big)\Big\}

\displaystyle=\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}})^{2}+\zeta_{\ell_{\star}}|\Xi_{u,m}|(-1)^{\ell_{\star}}(2\ell_{\star})!\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}\Big[\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}+1}^{\infty}\zeta_{j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2j}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\zeta^{*}_{\nu+j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2\nu+2j}\Big]

\displaystyle=\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}})^{2}+\theta\alpha^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}\xi_{\ell_{\star}}|\Xi_{u,m}|(-1)^{\ell_{\star}}(2\ell_{\star})!\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{F}_{u,d,\ell_{\star}}(\nu)+\theta\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg\{\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}+1}^{\infty}\alpha^{2j-2\nu}\xi_{j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2j}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\alpha^{2j}\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2\nu+2j}\Bigg\}.

(S.86)

For
\mathbf{k}_{1}\neq\mathbf{k}_{2}
, we use Lemma
S.9
to obtain that

\displaystyle\quad~\left\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\right\|^{2\nu}=\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left[k_{j}-k_{d+j}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right]^{2}\right\}^{\nu}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{d}(k_{j}-k_{d+j})^{2}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}^{\nu}=\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}\left\{\left[\sum_{j=1}^{d}(k_{j}-k_{d+j})^{2}\right]^{\nu}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\},

as
m\rightarrow\infty
uniformly over
\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}
.

For
\mathbf{k}_{1}=\mathbf{k}_{2}
, we have that

\displaystyle\quad~\left\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\right\|^{2\nu}

\displaystyle=\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left[\frac{\delta_{\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m};j}-ue_{j}-\delta_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m};j}}{m}\right]\right\}^{\nu}=uO\left(\frac{1}{m^{2\nu}}\right),

as
m\rightarrow\infty
uniformly over
\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}
. Therefore, by Lemma
S.9
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{u,d,\ell_{\star}}(\nu)

\displaystyle=\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\right\|^{2\nu}

\displaystyle=\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}:\mathbf{k}_{1}\neq\mathbf{k}_{2}}\left\{c_{d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left\{c_{d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}\left\{\left[\sum_{j=1}^{d}(k_{j}-k_{d+j})^{2}\right]^{\nu}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}:\mathbf{k}_{1}=\mathbf{k}_{2}}\left\{c_{d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left\{c_{d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}uO\left(\frac{1}{m^{2\nu}}\right)

\displaystyle=\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}\left\|\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{k}_{2}\right\|^{2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+\zeta^{*}_{\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|O\left\{\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}\omega_{m}^{-1}+u\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}\omega_{m}^{-2\nu}\right\}

\displaystyle=\theta\xi_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}+\theta\xi_{\nu}^{*}|\Xi_{u,m}|\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}O\left\{\omega_{m}^{-1}+u\omega_{m}^{-2\nu}\right\}

(S.87)

\displaystyle\asymp\theta m^{d}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu},

as
m\rightarrow\infty
, where
H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}
defined in (
15
) satisfies
H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}>0
, and
O(\cdot)
in the last equality only depends on
\nu
. Combining (
S7.1
) with (
S7.1
), (
S7.1
) and (
S7.1
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\quad~\mathbb{E}\left(V_{u,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)

\displaystyle=\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}\left(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\right)^{2}+\theta\alpha^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}\xi_{\ell_{\star}}|\Xi_{u,m}|(-1)^{\ell_{\star}}(2\ell_{\star})!\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad+\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}+\zeta^{*}_{\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}O\{\omega_{m}^{-1}+u\omega_{m}^{-2\nu}\}

\displaystyle\quad+\theta\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Bigg[\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}+1}^{\infty}\alpha^{2j-2\nu}\xi_{j}\left\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\right\|^{2j}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\alpha^{2j}\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}\left\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\right\|^{2\nu+2j}\Bigg]+O(1)\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}|\Xi_{u,m}|\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}

\displaystyle=\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}\left(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\right)^{2}+\theta\alpha^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}\xi_{\ell_{\star}}|\Xi_{u,m}|(-1)^{\ell_{\star}}(2\ell_{\star})!\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad+\theta\xi_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}+\theta\xi^{*}_{\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}O\left(\omega_{m}^{-1}+u\omega_{m}^{-2\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\theta\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|O(1)\left\{\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}+1}^{\infty}\xi_{j}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+O(1)\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}|\Xi_{u,m}|\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}},

(S.88)

as
m\rightarrow\infty
, and
O(\cdot)
only depends on
\ell_{\star},\nu,d,C_{\mathrm{f}}^{\prime}
and not on the parameters
\beta,\theta,\tau,\alpha
.

Hence, using the definitions of
C_{V,0},g_{\ell_{\star},\nu},H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}
in (
15
), we can derive from (
S7.1
) that when
u=1
,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}{\theta g_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}{\zeta_{\nu}^{*}(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m})^{2\nu}|\Xi_{1,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}

\displaystyle=1+O\left(\omega_{m}^{-1}+\omega_{m}^{-2\nu}\right)+O(1)\frac{\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}}{\theta}|\Xi_{u,m}|\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+O(1)\left[\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}}^{\infty}\frac{\xi_{j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j}\right]

(S.89)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}1+o(1),

as
m\to\infty
, where the relation (i) holds uniformly on the set
\Ecal_{n}
, because according to (
3.1
), for all
(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)\in\Ecal_{n}
, as
n\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\frac{\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}}{\theta}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}\leq\frac{p\|\beta\|^{2}}{\theta}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}\leq pn^{\rho_{1}-(2\ell_{\star}-2\nu)(1-\gamma)/d}=o(1),

\displaystyle\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\leq n^{\rho_{32}-(1-\gamma)/d}=o(1),

and that the two summations in (
S.89
) are all finite given the definition of
\xi_{j},\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}
for
j\in\mathbb{N}
in (
S7
). The
O(\cdot)
and
o(\cdot)
only depend on
\ell_{\star},\nu,d,C_{\mathrm{f}}^{\prime}
and not on
\beta,\theta,\tau,\alpha
, and the same applies to the derivation below as well.

For
u=0
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)-\tau C_{V,0}}{\zeta_{\nu}^{*}(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m})^{2\nu}|\Xi_{0,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}

\displaystyle=1+O\left(\omega_{m}^{-1}+\omega_{m}^{-2\nu}\right)+O(1)\frac{\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}}{\theta}|\Xi_{u,m}|\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+O(1)\left[\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}}^{\infty}\frac{\xi_{j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j}\right].

(S.90)

The summations in (
S.90
) are all finite given the definition of
\xi_{j},\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}
for
j\in\mathbb{N}
in (
S7
).

Furthermore, since as

,

\displaystyle\tau C_{V,0}

\displaystyle=\tau\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell}\left(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}\tau|\Xi_{0,m}|\left\{\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}\left(c_{d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\right)^{2}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}

(S.91)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{\asymp}}\tau m^{d},

where (i) follows from Lemma

and the

is uniform over all
\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}
; (ii) follows because the constants

\mathbf{i}
and

, and that

is a constant.

From (

) and (

), it follows that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}{\tau C_{V,0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)-\tau C_{V,0}}{\zeta_{\nu}^{*}(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m})^{2\nu}|\Xi_{0,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}\frac{\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{0,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}{\tau C_{V,0}}+1

\displaystyle=1+O(1)\frac{\theta}{\tau}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}\Bigg\{1+O\left(\omega_{m}^{-1}+\omega_{m}^{-2\nu}\right)+\frac{\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}}{\theta}|\Xi_{u,m}|\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell-2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+O(1)\left[\sum_{j=\ell}^{\infty}\frac{\xi_{j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j}\right]\Bigg\}

(S.92)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}1+o(1),

as
m\rightarrow\infty
, where the relation (i) holds uniformly on the set
\Ecal_{n}
, because according to (

), for all
(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)\in\Ecal_{n}
, as

,

\displaystyle\frac{\theta}{\tau}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}\leq n^{\rho_{21}-2\nu(1-\gamma)/d}=o(1),

\displaystyle\frac{\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}}{\theta}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}\leq\frac{p\|\beta\|^{2}}{\theta}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}\leq pn^{\rho_{1}-(2\ell_{\star}-2\nu)(1-\gamma)/d}=o(1),

\displaystyle\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\leq n^{\rho_{32}-(1-\gamma)/d}=o(1),

and that the two summations in (
S7.1
) are all finite given the definition of
\xi_{j},\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}
for

in (

).

Case A2.
If
\nu\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
, then the bound in (

) still applies, and we only need to derive the order for the second and the third terms in (

).

We observe that

\displaystyle\quad~\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}\left(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\right)^{2}+\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\theta K_{\alpha,\nu}\left(\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\right)

\displaystyle=\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}\left(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\right)^{2}+\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big\{\zeta_{j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2j}

\displaystyle\quad+\zeta^{*}_{\nu+j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2\nu+2j}\log\left(\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|\right)\Big\}

\displaystyle=\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}\left(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\right)^{2}+\zeta_{\ell_{\star}}|\Xi_{u,m}|(-1)^{\ell_{\star}}(2\ell_{\star})!\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big[\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2\nu}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\zeta^{*}_{\nu+1}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2\nu+2}

\displaystyle\quad\times\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\zeta^{*}_{\nu+1}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2\nu+2}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}+1}^{\infty}\zeta_{j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2j}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=2}^{\infty}\zeta^{*}_{\nu+j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2\nu+2j}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\log(\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|)\Big]

\displaystyle=\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}\left(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\theta\alpha^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}\{\xi_{1,\ell_{\star}}+\xi_{2,\ell_{\star}}\log(\alpha)\}|\Xi_{u,m}|(-1)^{\ell_{\star}}(2\ell_{\star})!\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{F}_{u,d,\ell_{\star}}(\nu)-\theta\alpha^{2}\xi^{*}_{\nu+1}|\Xi_{u,m}|(-1)^{\ell_{\star}}(2\ell_{\star})!\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\right)\Ical\{\ell_{\star}=\nu+1\}

\displaystyle\quad+\theta\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big[\alpha^{2}\xi^{*}_{\nu+1}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2\nu+2}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}+1}^{\infty}\alpha^{2j-2\nu}\xi_{1,j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2j}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}+1}^{\infty}\alpha^{2j-2\nu}\log(\alpha)\xi_{2,j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2j}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=2}^{\infty}\alpha^{2j}\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2\nu+2j}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\log\left(\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|\right)\Big].

(S.93)

Similar to Case A1, we can use Lemma
S.9
to derive the order for
\mathcal{F}_{u,d,\ell_{\star}}(\nu)
:

\displaystyle\quad~\mathcal{F}_{u,d,\ell_{\star}}(\nu)

\displaystyle=\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}}c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}c_{\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|^{2\nu}

\displaystyle\times\log\left(\|\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{k}_{1}\omega_{m})-\mathbf{s}(\mathbf{i}+u\mathbf{e}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}\omega_{m})\|\right)

\displaystyle=\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}:\mathbf{k}_{1}\neq\mathbf{k}_{2}}\left\{c_{d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}\left\{c_{d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left\{\left[\sum_{j=1}^{d}(k_{j}-k_{d+j})^{2}\right]^{\nu}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}\log\left\{\left[\sum_{j=1}^{d}(k_{j}-k_{d+j})^{2}\right]^{1/2}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle+\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{u,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{2d}\leq\ell_{\star}:\mathbf{k}_{1}=\mathbf{k}_{2}}\left\{c_{d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left\{c_{d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{d+1},\ldots,k_{2d})}+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})\right\}uO\left\{\left(\frac{1}{m}\right)^{2\nu}\log m\right\}

\displaystyle=\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}+\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|O\left\{\omega_{m}^{-1}+u\omega_{m}^{-2\nu}\log m\right\}

\displaystyle=\theta\xi_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}+\theta\xi_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|O(\omega_{m}^{-1}+u\omega_{m}^{-2\nu}\log m)

(S.94)

\displaystyle\asymp\theta m^{d}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu},

as
m\rightarrow\infty
. Combining (
S7.1
) with (
S7.1
), (
S7.1
) and (
S7.1
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\quad~\mathbb{E}\left(V_{u,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)

\displaystyle=\tau(1-u)\sum_{\mathbf{i}\in\Xi_{0,m}}\sum_{0\leq k_{1},\ldots,k_{d}\leq\ell_{\star}}\left(c_{\mathbf{i},d,\ell_{\star}}^{(k_{1},\ldots,k_{d})}\right)^{2}+\theta\xi_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+\theta\xi^{*}_{\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}O(\omega_{m}^{-1})+\theta\alpha^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}\{\xi_{1,\ell_{\star}}+\xi_{2,\ell_{\star}}\log(\alpha)\}|\Xi_{u,m}|(-1)^{\ell_{\star}}(2\ell_{\star})!\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}

\displaystyle\quad-\theta\alpha^{2}\xi^{*}_{\nu+1}|\Xi_{u,m}|(-1)^{\ell_{\star}}(2\ell_{\star})!\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\right)\Ical\{\ell_{\star}=\nu+1\}

\displaystyle\quad+\theta\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{u,m}|O(1)\Bigg\{\xi^{*}_{\nu+1}(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m})^{2}+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}+1}^{\infty}\xi_{1,j}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}+1}^{\infty}\log(\alpha)\xi_{2,j}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m})^{2j}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+O(1)\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}|\Xi_{u,m}|\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}},

(S.95)

as
m\rightarrow\infty
.

Hence, using the definitions of
C_{V,0},g_{\ell_{\star},\nu},H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}
in (

), we can derive from (

) that for

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}}{\theta g_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}V_{1,d,\ell_{\star}}}{\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{1,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}

\displaystyle=1+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})+O(1)\frac{\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}}{\theta}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+O(1)\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+1}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\right)\Ical\{\ell_{\star}=\nu+1\}

\displaystyle\quad+O(1)\Bigg[\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+1}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2}+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}}^{\infty}\frac{\xi_{1,j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}}^{\infty}\frac{\xi_{2,j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\log(\alpha)\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}+\sum_{j=2}^{\infty}\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\right)\Bigg]

(S.96)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}1+o(1),

where the last relation (i) holds uniformly on the set
\Ecal_{n}
, because according to (
3.1
), for all
(\theta,\alpha,\tau,\beta)\in\Ecal_{n}
, as

\displaystyle\frac{\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}}{\theta}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}\leq\frac{p\|\beta\|^{2}}{\theta}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}\leq pn^{\rho_{1}-(2\ell_{\star}-2\nu)(1-\gamma)/d}=o(1),

\displaystyle\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\leq n^{\rho_{32}-(1-\gamma)/d}=o(1),

\displaystyle\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2}|\log\alpha|\leq n^{2\rho_{32}-2(1-\gamma)/d}\cdot\frac{\rho_{31}+\rho_{32}}{d}\log n=o(1),

\displaystyle\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2(\ell_{\star}-\nu)}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\right)\leq n^{2(\ell_{\star}-\nu)[2\rho_{32}-2(1-\gamma)/d]}\cdot\frac{1-\gamma}{d}\log n=o(1),

(S.97)

and that the three summations in (
S.96
) are all finite given the definition of
\xi_{1,j},\xi_{2,j},\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}
for
j\in\mathbb{N}
in (
S7

For

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)-\tau C_{V,0}}{\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{0,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}

\displaystyle=1+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})+O(1)\frac{\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}}{\theta}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+O(1)\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+1}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\right)\Ical\{\ell_{\star}=\nu+1\}

\displaystyle\quad+O(1)\Bigg[\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+1}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2}+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}}^{\infty}\frac{\xi_{1,j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}}^{\infty}\frac{\xi_{2,j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\log(\alpha)\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}+\sum_{j=2}^{\infty}\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\right)\Bigg].

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)}{\tau C_{V,0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(V_{0,d,\ell_{\star}}\right)-\tau C_{V,0}}{\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{0,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}\frac{\zeta_{\nu}^{*}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}|\Xi_{0,m}|H_{\ell_{\star},\nu}}{\tau C_{V,0}}+1

\displaystyle=1+O(1)\frac{\theta}{\tau}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}\Bigg\{1+O(\omega_{m}^{-1})+O(1)\frac{\|\beta\|_{1}^{2}}{\theta}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\ell_{\star}-2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+O(1)\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+1}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\right)\Ical\{\ell_{\star}=\nu+1\}

\displaystyle\quad+O(1)\Bigg[\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+1}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2}+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}}^{\infty}\frac{\xi_{1,j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=\ell_{\star}}^{\infty}\frac{\xi_{2,j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\log(\alpha)\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j-2\nu}+\sum_{j=2}^{\infty}\frac{\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}}{\xi^{*}_{\nu}}\left(\alpha\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2j}\log\left(\frac{m}{\omega_{m}}\right)\Bigg]\Bigg\}

(S.98)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}1+o(1),

as

, where the relation (i) holds uniformly on the set
\Ecal_{n}
because of the relations in (
S7.1
) on
\Ecal_{n}
,

\displaystyle\frac{\theta}{\tau}\left(\frac{\omega_{m}}{m}\right)^{2\nu}\leq n^{\rho_{21}-2\nu(1-\gamma)/d}=o(1)

on
\Ecal_{n}
, and that the three summations in (
S.98
) are all finite given the definition of
\xi_{1,j},\xi_{2,j},\xi^{*}_{\nu+j}
for

in (
